- **rsd:walk**: Start a walk — a living tasklist you step through one item at a time via /rsd:next. Captures items from recent conversation (with confirmation) or from the argument, plus a clear intent line so no item gets discussed in a vacuum. ([snovis/rsd](https://github.com/snovis/rsd/blob/HEAD/commands/walk.md))
